Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The biodiversity variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 954 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 541 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by biodiversity generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 954, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 541. QED.
